## MULTICTL-0681 / CONTROLLER REGISTRY IDENTITY ISOLATION — IN PROGRESS — 2026-10-09 Asia/Manila

- **Baseline evidence:** SHA `82de7cf22597f845ac1648bedce286c79190f94b`, PR #30 DRAFT. Same-SHA Full #37942714286, Windows SoftTimer #37942714335, native journal #37942714217 and three-target official OpenWrt LAB SDK #37942714238 all COMPLETED SUCCESS. PEVID-0680 structural test is done; **not** a physical power-cut trial.
- **P0 LAB finding (source verified):** `tools/v060_journal_authority_native.c:getkey()` verifies a duplicate controller ID only when it matches the *requested* ID. It never checks if two different controller identities share the same 32-byte HMAC key, and does not reject a zero-valued secret. ## AUTH-0663 / IN PROGRESS — 2026-10-09 Asia/Manila — revoke old admin sessions BEFORE password credential commit

- **P0 ordering residual from AUTH-0662:** full `cgi-bin/admin` and R281 `cgi-bin/rental-admin` currently call `bp_auth_set_password` to persist a new credential then call checked `bp_auth_invalidate_user_sessions`. If revocation fails, the response now correctly returns error but already-committed new password may coexist with still-active old session tokens; attackers holding old tokens may retain privileges. This is a real authenticated security flaw despite truthful error ACK.
- **Scope/decision:** under existing auth lock and after minimum password-length validation, invalidate the old user sessions **before** committing new password, so failed invalidation leaves existing password unchanged; if password write subsequently fails, sessions are revoked but old password remains usable for a fresh login (fail closed, recoverable). Preserve login role/CSRF checks and password-change audit. CLI `auth.sh --set-password|--set-bootstrap` is also a privileged credential mutation and must revoke that user's sessions before writing. Add source regression verifying order and real simulated no-write-on-failed-revocation; preserve 9-field existing session schema, no new live tokens generated.
- **Risks/rollback:** old session may be invalidated for an invalid password change; prevalidate length before revoke and document any remaining error. No live owner signer/password/customer data or frozen alpha.3 changed. Existing AUTH-0662 P0 test [#37889006858](https://github.com/BlazingSystems/BlazingSystems-Experiments/actions/runs/37889006858) SUCCESS; latest canonical docs HEAD `1f6649e9f88322a44f617f8103507a2ad69524ed`, exact Full+Windows were queued/in progress; follow-up source supersedes their SHA. ## AUTH-0661 / IN PROGRESS — 2026-10-09 Asia/Manila — admin password database partial-write containment

- **P0 source audit:** `openwrt/rootfs/usr/lib/blazepwifi/auth.sh:bp_auth_set_password` and `profiles/r281-rental/root/usr/lib/blazepwifi/auth.sh` first copy all existing admin user verifier rows via unchecked `awk`, append a new verifier via unchecked `printf`, and may rename a truncated file even when the first stage has failed. `chmod/mv` can fail but trailing `bp_durable_sync` and audit hide failures, so reset may be reported successful despite no stored verifier or lost other operators. Preserve existing RNG-0660 missing entropy tests and must not regress standalone login.

## RNG-0659 + RNG-0660 / VERIFIED FOCUSED CI — 2026-10-09 Asia/Manila — repair broken primary admin auth and reject failed entropy

- **P0 live-source finding:** `openwrt/rootfs/usr/lib/blazepwifi/auth.sh` in source HEAD `9bb9f1b322325bea498d59b7aa8abe64c1a82485` was malformed by an incomplete RNG-0659 text edit: regex `grep -Eq '^[a-f0-9]+` ended in an unterminated quote at line 61, and a complete second copy of almost the entire auth file followed the first CLI `esac`, causing POSIX sh `|| unexpected` at line 286 even after repairing only the first function. This was ACTUAL CI failure, not cosmetic: [fast P0 #37886251548](https://github.com/BlazingSystems/BlazingSystems-Experiments/actions/runs/37886251548) **FAILURE** at authenticated rental admin bootstrap `auth.sh:508: Unterminated quoted string`; partial repair `acfef9cd69599630fc87826b1c3e241becb2a230` copied known-good standalone R281 CSPRNG block but failed next fast [#37887555144](https://github.com/BlazingSystems/BlazingSystems-Experiments/actions/runs/37887555144), `auth.sh:286: || unexpected`. Full repair `e57619ce7997ed4432e9429c98d8442b5c8ab98b` truncated duplicate suffix after exactly one complete CLI dispatch; primary library is 11178 bytes, with one random_hex, login and clean_field function each.
- **New security CI trigger:** `.github/workflows/blazepwifi-p0-repro.yml` `d4746053ba304167d731c655914e43d2dc35a5db` includes primary `auth.sh` in source paths and `sh -n` on BOTH R281/full libraries before signed-payment tests. Previously R281 changed triggered test, but full auth source alone did not trigger focused P0.

## RNG-0660 / IN_PROGRESS — 2026-10-09 Asia/Manila — fail closed if credential/session CSPRNG fails

- **P0 confirmed security flaw:** Both `openwrt/rootfs/usr/lib/blazepwifi/auth.sh` and `profiles/r281-rental/root/usr/lib/blazepwifi/auth.sh` call `bp_auth_random_hex` to generate an admin password salt and two admin session secrets but do not test its exit status. RNG-0659 now correctly fails if no CSPRNG is present; without propagating that error a password can be saved with empty salt or an authenticated admin session with empty/predictable session token/CSRF fields. Session creation/logging may also be ACKed after an unchecked append/chmod failure.
- **Scope & plan:** change BOTH libs to reject missing/short invalid salt/session/CSRF before any password mutation or session append; return nonzero from session creation if append/chmod fails; stop `bp_auth_login` before success audit/session when token generation failed. Add `tests/v060_auth_rng_failclosed.sh` with synthetic source libraries and a deterministic failure stub; verify no new admin user/empty sessions/false success, normal crypto path still returns proper secrets. Wire focused P0 and Full. No owner Android signing key, customer password, or active session rotated on devices.
- **Dependency/failure retained:** Full auth library was discovered corrupted at `9bb9f1b322...`: RNG-0659 function ended with truncated quote at line 61 and a duplicated ~9KB suffix after first `esac`. Partial repair `acfef9cd69599630fc87826b1c3e241becb2a230` still carried suffix and fast Actions #37887555144 FAILED `auth.sh syntax error || unexpected`. Complete removal of duplicate tail `e57619ce7997ed4432e9429c98d8442b5c8ab98b` plus `.github/workflows/blazepwifi-p0-repro.yml` syntax/preflight source path `d4746053ba304167d731c655914e43d2dc35a5db`; new focused #37887643286 in progress, no success claim. Previous broken run #37886251548 FAILED syntactic unterminated quote. Full+Windows at intermediary source fail handover freshness until 3-file doc synchronization.
- **Safety:** code-only development PR #30, `PRODUCTION_v0.6.0_RELEASED=0`, immutable public alpha.3 at source 3a4ee7c, no hardware acceptance. Next source change affects both auth libraries only. Requires exact CI proof, then full handover checkpoint and newest Full/Windows review. **NEXT EXACT ACTION:** implement error propagation for both auth libs + synthetic test, check focused accounting CI, commit and sync handovers without deleting failure history.

## RNG-0659 / IN PROGRESS — 2026-10-09 Asia/Manila — full OpenWrt QR/admin/session token entropy parity

Add P0 and Full CI proof. Do not rotate stored original production signing identity or customer admin keys.

- **P0/P1 real CI source finding:** an unrelated MIG-0656 binding regression triggered old signed `ENROLL-0655` test intermittently, failing at `fault-policy` and `fault-devices`. The caller now detects short keys, exposing underlying generator weakness. Root R281 path differs from hardened full-profile generator.

## ENROLL-0655 / IN PROGRESS — 2026-10-09 Asia/Manila — R281 signed Android QR enrollment must be one-time and acknowledged only after identity is persisted

No `bp_lock` surrounds token lookup/auth/write/consume, permitting two concurrent installations from one one-time token. The actual R281 `rental.sh:bp_rental_enroll_consume` does not verify exactly one valid token was consumed; `bp_rental_policy_ensure` appends unchecked partial state.
- **Bounded fix:** keep HMAC/QR protocol unchanged, serialize one-token enrollment under `bp_lock` and existing global `bp_paid_begin/commit` uncertainty marker; check secure random IDs, identity already absent, durable device/policy/one-time-token consumption success before any JSON success or credential return. On a partial write/consume, preserve paid-state marker and return error for operator reconciliation; never auto-clear it or mint duplicate secret. Harden policy ensure and token consume against source corruption, symlink, failed copy/rename. Add isolated signed actual R281 CGI tests for first enrollment, second replay denial, malformed source, failed device/policy/token rename, marker+no false ACK.
- **Safety:** this is v1 conservative containment, NOT a crash-atomic identity/paid journal, NOT verified factory-reset Android Device Owner QR, and NOT a production updater. Only draft PR #30; immutable LAB alpha.3 source 3a4ee7c, existing customers, signer and release assets untouched. Code/test CI must be verified at exact commit before concluding. `PRODUCTION_0.6.0_RELEASED=0`.
- **Rollback:** revert new scoped R281 source/fixture commits without clearing any real `paid-state-uncertain` marker or deleting customer identity; operator recovery after partially committed enrollment requires auditable state reconciliation.

## RENT-0650 / IN PROGRESS — 2026-10-09 Asia/Manila — eliminate rental heartbeat paid-time stale overwrite

- **P0 confirmed race:** authenticated `cgi-bin/rental:status|policy_get` reads `rental-devices.tsv` (including paid lease), performs session/redeemed-token cleanup and policy/inventory work, then **rewrites the entire device row using the OLD read lease** just to update `last_seen`. A concurrent signed Vendo coin credit or operator time grant can commit between read and write, then a legitimate Android status poll overwrites newly purchased rental time while returning `ok:true`. Global `bp_lock` around enrollment cleanup does not cover this later paid-row rewrite. Frequent status polls also unnecessarily write sensitive flash accounting state.
- **Safe design:** stop rewriting the paid device row on STATUS/policy_get entirely. Add `bp_rental_seen_update` / `bp_rental_seen_get` to `rental.sh`, store only numeric last-seen in private, disposable `BP_RUN/rental-last-seen/<device-id>` (atomic temp rename; no paid state, no flash commit), validate 24-hex device identity and target symlink/file, show volatile value on `rental_device_list`/admin JSON with persisted old value as fallback. After heartbeat update, re-read server-authoritative current device secret and lease for response signature so phone cannot be told a persisted stale lease. This is *not* an authorization to change paid time or ignore authenticity failures. Keep signed response and enrollment status behavior stable.
- **Proof:** add real signed rental status CGI synthetic test proving identical hash of `rental-devices.tsv` before/after status, failure-injected paid-store rename cannot break status, volatile last_seen updates admin listing, and new paid lease visible after subsequent status. Add P0+Full CI regression and legacy rental tests. Never touch customer devices/keys or frozen lab alpha.3.
- **Remaining limits:** other signed admin time grants (`bp_rental_lease_add/expire`) and revoke need latest lock-order race review; authentic physical powercuts and unified fsync v2 journal still P0. Start only dev PR #30 and keep 0.6 updater refused. **NEXT EXACT ACTION:** source+test+Actions+canonical three-file checkpoint; no production claim.

## RENT-0649 / IN PROGRESS — 2026-10-09 Asia/Manila — prohibit silent paid-time loss in admin set-duration

- **P0 direct source:** `cgi-bin/admin:rental_lease_set` unconditionally computes `lease_until=now+seconds` and overwrites an existing lease even if it shortens unconsumed purchased seconds; authenticated operator with CSRF needs a separate explicit settlement intent for this loss. Existing path does use paid-state quarantine and storage checks, but omits a rental-event entry for old→new lease and does not acquire `bp_lock` before reading the previous balance, allowing a race with a signed Vendo credit event.
- **Scope/plan:** under `bp_lock` and `bp_paid_begin`, re-read current paid lease, reject shorter duration if `confirm_forfeit != CONFIRM_FORFEIT`; require proper non-negative numeric snapshot. On explicit shortening audit `lease_set` with old and new expiry and forfeited seconds before `bp_paid_commit` success ACK; on any failed lease storage/audit stage quarantine marker remains and HTTP returns `ok:false`, never silently overwrite. Keep absolute set-duration functionality for intentional admin actions, existing CSRF, role and HTTPS checks. Add source-backed dynamic/admin synthetic test (with existing auth fixtures if available) and require P0+Full. No live data or released alpha.3 touched.

## FSYNC-0643 / IN PROGRESS — 2026-10-09 Asia/Manila — native durable snapshot replacement proof

- **P0 target primitive gap:** currently v2 receipt+balances synthetic `tools/v060_journal_fixture.sh` writes a single SHA256 snapshot through shell `mv`+`sync` and production v1 uses separate TSVs. Neither proves `fsync(source fd)`, `renameat` and `fsync(parent-directory fd)` ordering on actual target flash, and a lost ACK may occur after a successful rename but before durable directory commit.
- **Scope:** add separately compiled **synthetic ONLY**, uninstalled `tools/v060_durable_replace.c` Linux/POSIX userspace syscall helper, which accepts only a specially marked disposable `/tmp/blaze-v2-atomic-*/` directory and two strictly validated simple basenames; rejects symlink/hardlink/unsafe path and malformed sentinel; obtains dirfd, opens/validates private source, `fsync(src)`, `renameat(dirfd,src,dirfd,dst)`, `fsync(dirfd)`, and prints ACK only on all success. Fault injection compiled under `BLAZE_FIXTURE_ONLY` before source flush, before rename and after rename before dirsync must refuse or fail-with-uncertainty, no success ACK.
- **Test:** `tests/v060_durable_replace_native.sh` builds with `cc -std=c11 -Wall -Wextra -Werror -D_GNU_SOURCE -DBLAZE_FIXTURE_ONLY` to temporary sandbox; tests old snapshot preserved before rename, after rename uncertain no ACK, retry semantics must be external replay, symlinks and bad marker rejected, successful src/directory fsync and rename; do not modify customer balances. Wire dedicated P0 and Full, collect genuine CI.
- **Limitations:** This fixture is NOT an installed runtime ledger, does not authenticate a monetary event, and does not prove `fsync` on Ruijie NAND, Orange Pi SD/eMMC, or x86 ext4/f2fs/overlayfs without physical power-cut tests and verified firmware toolchain. - **NEXT ACTION:** commit C helper + adversarial native test, require targeted and Full CI, verify exact success, reconcile handovers, then develop a guarded v2 provider integrating authentic controller receipt/sequence/balance only after proper migration and signer strategy.

## ACC-0642 / IN PROGRESS — 2026-10-09 Asia/Manila — Wi-Fi paid account snapshot corruption refusal

- **Source-discovered P0:** `openwrt/rootfs/usr/lib/blazepwifi/common.sh:bp_account_write` currently uses `awk` to replace/create one `accounts.tsv` row and returns success even when existing rows are duplicated or malformed: a formerly corrupted/pretruncated credit row survives, and a duplicate same-device ID receives two updates, potentially creating non-deterministic credit/read behavior across independent endpoints. Existing `bp_paid_begin/commit` global quarantine unchanged. No automatic damage recovery.
- **Scope/compatibility:** current account schema is 9 TSV fields `device,credit,expiry,remaining,paused,pause_started,mac,ip,events`; legacy `credits.tsv`/`sessions.tsv` are distinct migration sources, never reinterpret them as accounts.tsv. Do NOT claim transactional all-or-nothing of write+receipt on power loss; previously existing P0 root remains.

## PAY-0635 / IN PROGRESS — 2026-10-09 Asia/Manila — signed member transfer/bank receipt payload collision

- **P0 accounting protocol:** audited `openwrt/rootfs/www/blazepwifi/cgi-bin/vendo` `member_bank` and `member_transfer` replay branches, plus `openwrt/rootfs/usr/lib/blazepwifi/member.sh` `bp_member_balance_change` and `bp_member_transfer`. Existing receipt lookup identifies same event ID/controller/username/type but *does not compare previously acknowledged amount*; transfer additionally fails to compare the stored destination. Re-signed reuse of event_id with a new amount/destination may wrongly ACK that new intent without changing balance, creating disputed money and incorrect client state even when `paid-state-uncertain` marker is absent.
- **Scoped fix:** fail closed on previously retained ID where amount differs, and for transfer destination where stored receipt detail (`source_new_balance:destination:destination_new_balance`) differs; preserve exact matching replay. Enforce in both core member financial functions (admin/other callers) and Vendo signed replay fast path; do not touch existing customer data, version/signing or frozen lab alpha.2.
- **Required test:** use real signed `cgi-bin/vendo` in `tests/v060_member_quarantine_replay.sh` synthetic temporary fixture to prove matching bank/transfer replay succeeds, changed amount or transfer target returns `ok:false`, no money/receipt mutation, existing paid-state quarantine still works. Add focused P0+Full CI.
- **Safety:** source PR #30 only, no production rollout, no 0.6 upgrade guard removal. Full source v1 migration, atomic ledger and physical tests remain blocked. Status `IN_PROGRESS`.
- **NEXT EXACT ACTION:** patch core and Vendo exact replay comparisons, extend synthetic regression, run test and record actual Actions SHA/conclusion in all handovers.

## PAY-0630 / IN PROGRESS — 2026-10-09 Asia/Manila — live coin target replay retention

- **Priority P0.** `common.sh` `bp_events_push` enforces `event_history` (typically 64, synthetic stress 8) by evicting older `c:` coin event IDs even when the original coin target nonce remains valid for 120+ seconds. `cgi-bin/vendo` deduplication relies only on `bp_find_event_device` scanning that bounded list. Repeating an older signed controller nonce after enough new events may double-credit the same cash pulse while active target is unchanged.
- **Scoped plan:** encode new v0.6 development coin receipt IDs as `c:<target_nonce>.<sha256(coin|controller|nonce|target)>` so target expiry can be assessed; `bp_events_push` retains all events for *any currently live verified hotspot target*, bounding only expired/legacy records by `event_history`. Voucher writes also use this function and must not evict live coin receipts. Reject unexpectedly oversized receipt lists BEFORE money write, with no ACK; log synthetic live-CGI test (>8 signed coins, replay oldest, plus voucher during same window).
- **Legacy caveat:** v0.5's `c:<hash>` tokens cannot be retrospectively linked to a target; migration must quiesce old windows and retain safe historical paid state, not silently reinterpret old events. This development change does NOT yet constitute a crash-atomic journal, durable powercut proof or approved v1 conversion.
- **PAY-0629 evidence:** source `1fefddfb759f71e40f14caf705a705f59679e7d8` + `fc5cd194eff6f5e58a5956aa31069a8d19deab6c` + test `11e26989ef573c1976c73b7f5c1d4d984bfad5f6`; targeted P0 [#37836724942](https://github.com/BlazingSystems/BlazingSystems-Experiments/actions/runs/37836724942) **SUCCESS** exact test SHA. New cross-branch Full/Windows still need exact-head gate after docs checkpoint.
- **Safety:** PR #30 source only; no customer updates, no historical release replacement, v0.6 updater still refuses; production P0 journal/migration/signer/physical remain blocked.
- **NEXT EXACT ACTION:** add exact signed-CGI replay/eviction regression and source target-aware pruning, run focused P0 and existing stress/integration tests, synchronize canonical handovers after actual results.

## PAY-0627 / IN PROGRESS — 2026-10-09 Asia/Manila — live Wi-Fi coin false-ACK containment

- **Priority P0 money loss, branch** `blazepwifi-v0.6.0-audit-foundation`, PR #30. Unlike member/rental paid paths, `openwrt/rootfs/www/blazepwifi/cgi-bin/vendo` ordinary Wi-Fi coin branch calls `bp_account_write` without checking its return and without `bp_paid_begin/bp_paid_commit`. `common.sh` `bp_account_write` also ends in `bp_durable_sync`, masking a failed `awk > tmp` or `mv` when durable_sync=0. ## SEC-0626 / IN PROGRESS — 2026-10-09 (Asia/Manila) — updater archive extraction hardening

- **Priority P1, scope:** `openwrt/rootfs/usr/lib/blazepwifi/update.sh` and synthetic `tests/v060_update_archive_security.sh`, Full CI validation step. Branch `blazepwifi-v0.6.0-audit-foundation` PR #30. Keep 0.6 upgrade rejection, v0.5.x compatibility, customer finances and existing releases unmodified.
- **Observed risk:** `bp_update_safe_archive` only inspects `tar -tzf` path strings; archive symlink/hardlink/special members can be extracted before payload verification. It also allows duplicate entry names and `manifest.tsv` duplicated target paths. A verified SHA of the tarball alone does not make malicious archive contents safe.
- **Planned bounded fix:** validate an allowlisted archive member namespace, reject all nonregular/nondirectory member types (especially links), path traversal/double slash/dot components/duplicate names; require well-formed unique manifest destinations; test using a temporary fake filesystem with valid, symlink and duplicate bundles. Preserve all existing test gates.
- **Tests:** exact source test `sh tests/v060_update_archive_security.sh`, v051/v052/v060 migration/rollback compatibility and new Full CI. Record actual conclusion+SHA. **State IN_PROGRESS, no pass claim.**
- **Rollback:** revert scoped code+test+workflow commits if compatibility fails; source `VERSION=0.5.3`, production `0.6` preflight fail-closed. **Production blocker still active:** P0 paid journal/old money migration/current+rescue signed Android/hardware 30 devices.
- **NEXT EXACT ACTION:** update the archive validator before `tar -xzf`, add regression fixtures and Full validate workflow test, then synchronize all canonical handovers with observed CI.

## MIG-0625 / IN PROGRESS — 2026-10-09 Asia/Manila — synthetic v2 sequence canonicalization

- **Scope:** LAB-ONLY `tools/v060_journal_fixture.sh` and `tests/v060_journal_native_fixture.sh`, branch `blazepwifi-v0.6.0-audit-foundation` / draft PR #30. No production server money files, admin endpoints, Android signer, installed systems, or released tags affected.
- **Bug evidence from source:** `seq=025`, `event=controller:025` passes numeric comparison; the AWK ledger serializes receipt sequence as `25` but keeps event as `controller:025`. On the next call, receipt verification demands `controller:25`, so the synthetic ledger can fail closed permanently after a supposedly successful commit. Potentially similar ambiguous leading-zero spellings should be rejected BEFORE any write.
- **Planned fix:** enforce canonical decimal `seq` (no leading zero), and test refused `025` with unchanged ledger/hash and a subsequent valid `25` command. Preserve conservative replay/stale-sequence checks and explicit nonproduction warnings.
- **Test/acceptance:** existing native fixture test + new leading-zero regression; exact-HEAD GitHub Actions if triggered, record run ID only after verified. Rollback by reverting two bounded source/test file edits. **State: in_progress; no pass yet.**
- **NEXT EXACT ACTION:** reject noncanonical positive sequence at the CLI boundary in `tools/v060_journal_fixture.sh`, add explicit no-mutation regression to `tests/v060_journal_native_fixture.sh`, run tests and synchronize all three canonical handover documents. Production `SUCCESS_REPORT=0`, still blocked by authenticated runtime ledger/migration/Lineage-2 signing and real hardware acceptance.

## V060-RENTAL-PIN / P0 — IN PROGRESS 2026-10-09: managed Android binding cannot silently drop TLS pin

- **Source issue:** Android `android/BlazeRentalLauncher/src/com/blazesystems/blazerental/LeaseClient.java:applyPinnedTls` returns immediately when stored cert pin is empty, even if `ManagedPolicyController.isDeviceOwner(context)` reports true. Back-end Device Owner provisioning QR requires admin cert pin, but a malformed/local state transition or alternate enrollment path could allow an unpinned managed client to initiate requests; this violates higher-security rental deployment intent. Ordinary manual APK remains lower-security by design.
- **Change scope:** in `LeaseClient.applyPinnedTls`, require nonempty valid pin *whenever DeviceOwner is active*, fail closed before HTTP connection, leaving the existing manual-install unpinned fallback explicit and unaffected. Add source security contract test `tests/v060_managed_tls_pin.sh` that asserts the guard and preserves separate QR options, and require it in Full `validate`. No enrollment secrets, actual user data, managed state or APK signature touched.
- **Risk/compatibility:** owners who installed Device Owner outside the official pinned QR might lose connectivity; this is intentional fail-safe, with on-device administrative repair/re-provisioning required. Cannot claim no-bypass or signed APK release until hardware-specific test. Rollback by restoring guarded method and removing test; never insert debug trust-all or TLS bypass.
- **Success:** source test verifies managed guard presence, no empty-pin early return in all modes, normal pinned certificate checks intact. Android Gradle and relevant Android source tests must pass at exact HEAD; no production signing claim.
- **NEXT ACTION:** implement narrow conditional, CI, inspect source test and Android build, synchronize all handovers and UX research audit. Production 0.6.0 publication still gated by v1 migration, durable P0 paid journal, signer and hardware proof.

- **Intent:** Fix one narrow, presently demonstrated runtime defect in issue #31: `member.sh:bp_member_event_record` currently truncates the most recent N events indiscriminately, which forgets accepted paid IDs and permits replay. This cannot recover paid events already evicted by old versions; migration must separately reconcile them and 0.6 release remains blocked. Do not touch Standalone/profile, signing or released assets.
- **Affected source:** `openwrt/rootfs/usr/lib/blazepwifi/member.sh`, `tests/v060_member_replay_repro.sh` and isolated `.github/workflows/blazepwifi-p0-repro.yml` (change only the replay expectation to GREEN once verified), Full build required `validate` regression step. Do not change receipt EIO tests/expected RED or rental state.
- **Risks:** financial audit grows to quota; the correct safe behavior is reject before new paid balance mutation and require operator maintenance, not trim. Pending ACK/crash atomicity remains unresolved; accepted old IDs might be missing. Event-history file itself is not an authenticated durable journal; replay shield is incremental only. I/O errors during append and early power loss still require later v2 journal repair.
- **Success proof:** existing fixture after 129 nonfinancial events MUST retain original paid receipt and give replay without extra 10 seconds; stage/full tests include unchanged member receipt-error/rental receipt-error intentionally RED; assert capped nonfinancial count, financial receipt preservation and preflight cap. No physical field claims.
- **Rollback:** source/test rollback on dev branch only; no live DB migration. **NEXT:** implement narrow retention+preflight change, adjust regression/CI after verified fix, synchronize all handovers and inspect exact-head CI. Production release still denied per `docs/RELEASE_DECISION_0.6.0.md`.

## Non-negotiable invariants

1. No transactions are silently duplicated, erased, converted or unaccounted. Concurrent coinslots must not cross-credit sessions, even through ACK loss and power cuts.
2. Server-authoritative time, pause, membership and rental policy; Windows local/remote mode authority explicitly distinguished. A 250ms callback may never charge an entire second.
3. No generic universal production admin password, secret logging or source-upload of recovery keys. Secure session/CSRF/rate-limiting and role enforcement must persist across all web routes.
4. **Binding QR** is an existing-app enrollment token, lower security. **Device Owner QR** is factory setup provisioning plus pinned TLS, signed APK and verified hash; neither is interchangeable. Avoid falsely promising zero bypass.
5. Dedicated `profiles/standalone-rental` and released tags stay untouched. Both standard and centralized PisoNet modes remain supported by separate Windows source tree.
6. All firmware/installer builds must use the intended physical revision and safe rollback; preserve Windows EXE as an EXE, not a PowerShell-only handoff.
7. Run tests on exact head, review complete artifacts, and never claim green/released when critical job is failed, skipped, canceled or pending.
8. Keep this document, `PROJECT_HANDOVER.md` top status and `CHANGE_LEDGER.md` synchronized **after each meaningful change**. New chat reads this file first after root handover.

## MIG-0610 — P0 upgrade-transaction safety work (IN PROGRESS)

- **Finding from source 2026-10-08:** `openwrt/rootfs/usr/lib/blazepwifi/update.sh` applies payload and subsequently runs `update-migrate.sh "$previous" "$version" || true`, so failed migration is ignored and can be promoted as healthy. Current rollback snapshot saves only manifest-listed system files; it does **not** automatically revert mutations to `/etc/config/blazepwifi`, `/etc/blazepwifi/state`, member banks or rental identities. `update-migrate.sh` already changes UCI configuration. ## Verified exact-source MIG-0610 result and new SYS-0612 P0 pre-change record

- **MIG-0610 VERIFIED in development CI:** [BlazePwifi run #37776446840](https://github.com/BlazingSystems/BlazingSystems-Experiments/actions/runs/37776446840) **COMPLETED SUCCESS** on `541282869753dc2b64058c45a162ab4b96e83be9`: 26 jobs passed, 0 failed, `validate` included v0.6 migration fail-closed regression and v0.5.x compatibility, Android Device Owner emulator, Ruijie/Orange Pi/x86 simulation and candidate gate. [Windows #37776446835](https://github.com/BlazingSystems/BlazingSystems-Experiments/actions/runs/37776446835) SUCCESS both jobs on identical SHA. This is the last green *source* checkpoint, not a 0.6.0 signed release.
- **SYS-0612 / P0 / RESEARCH CONFIRMED — IN PROGRESS:** OpenWrt documentation explicitly warns that sysupgrade wipes custom files not included in its preserve set. Current BlazePwifi `openwrt/rootfs/` has no `lib/upgrade/keep.d/blazepwifi` or `etc/sysupgrade.conf` declaration. Full firmware sysupgrade could therefore fail to retain `/etc/blazepwifi/state` (paid accounts, vouchers, member banks, rental identity/keys, remote keys), `/etc/blazepwifi/portal`, and the pinned local TLS certificate/key. This is distinct from the overlay updater and a **NO-GO for field firmware release**. Official references: https://openwrt.org/docs/guide-user/installation/generic.sysupgrade and https://openwrt.org/docs/techref/sysupgrade.
- **Intended bounded fix:** add package-owned `openwrt/rootfs/lib/upgrade/keep.d/blazepwifi` entries for `/etc/config/blazepwifi`, `/etc/blazepwifi/state`, `/etc/blazepwifi/portal`, `/etc/uhttpd.crt` and `/etc/uhttpd.key`; allow **only this exact file** in `bp_update_path_allowed` so deployed 0.5.x overlay upgrade installs preservation policy. Never overwrite an operator's `/etc/sysupgrade.conf`, and do not auto-include huge `/etc/blazepwifi/update/snapshots` in tiny-device sysupgrade archives. Add tests for critical paths, whitelist scope and inclusion in generated overlay bundle.
- **Remaining unknowns:** on-device `sysupgrade -l` file list, actual payload size, hardware flash availability, boot/restore after factory flash, local HTTPS fingerprint continuity, and private off-device backup. Those require physical verification and must not be claimed green based on source-only testing.
- **NEXT:** commit only scoped preserve-list and whitelist, add mandatory tests, synchronize root/current/ledger, run exact-head CI. Do not upgrade live devices or publish signed 0.6.0 binaries.

## UI-FUSION-0614 real Chromium failure diagnosis — P1 (IN PROGRESS)

- On exact SHA 75d88c87b844d1d3f73469ff757317847234d303, BlazePwifi Actions #37799066752 job v04_browser_simulation ID 113386892960 FAILED. Full browser audit passed first; separate standalone_fusion_browser.py line 91 asserted document.scrollWidth <= 390+2 and failed with `AssertionError: Standalone rental page overflows mobile viewport 390`. Source/theme static validate PASS. No backend/security/money failure. Artifact #11559034502 contains partial browser evidence.
- Root cause under investigation: small-screen Standalone CSS uses a 12-track `repeat(12,1fr)` grid and auto min-content sizing, which can expand grid items beyond viewport. Planned fix: change small-screen tracks to minmax(0,1fr), bound flex/grid min-width and use explicit content wrapping; *never mask real overflow via overflow-x:hidden*. Improve regression error output to print offending bounding-box element selectors/widths if any persist.
- NEXT: patch only standalone HTML responsive CSS and browser diagnostics, then resync root/current/ledger, run latest exact-head browser test, confirm 360 and 390 mobile and original rentals. All production releases frozen.
